UI action record and replay\\n\\n## The problem\\n\\nA model can work out how to complete a flow in a web app. That is not the capability worth shipping.\\nThe capability worth shipping is a **recording that replays deterministically, with no model in the\\nloop** — because a workflow that needs the model on every run is a demo with a subscription.\\n\\nYou are given a recorded action The recorded node id is a hint, not an address. |\\n| `R2_PRECONDITION_OBSERVED` | Before a step runs, its precondition must be OBSERVED — read the attribute, do not assume the recorded value still holds. The audit records what was observed. |\\n| `R3_POSTCONDITION_OBSERVED` | After a step runs, its postcondition is recorded from what the application shows, not from what the recording expected. |\\n| `R4_CONFIRMATION_OBSERVED` | A step whose target declares `data-opens-confirmation` must not complete until a confirmation has been observed and accepted. Read-only — the grader uses these exact shapes.\\n\\n// The PUBLIC domain model for UI action record-and-replay.\\n//\\n// Shipped verbatim in the agent-facing package, so nothing here is ground truth. The scenario, its\\n// mutations and the effect ledger live in `truth.ts`.\\n//\\n// THE THESIS\\n//\\n// A model can discover a UI workflow. That is not the capability worth having. The capability worth\\n// having is a RECORDING that replays deterministically with no model in the loop — because a\\n// workflow that needs the model every time is not automation, it is a demo with a subscription.\\n//\\n// So the task is not \\\"click the right thing\\\". It is: record a trace whose every step carries what\\n// must be true before it and what must be true after it, then replay that trace against a UI that\\n// has changed underneath you, and either complete it or halt with a named reason. Improvising is a\\n// failure even when it works — `R6_NO_MODEL_IN_LOOP` is a rule, and the harness owns the channel\\n// that would let you break it.\\n\\nexport interface UiNode {\\n  readonly id: string;\\n  readonly role: string;\\n  readonly attrs: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;\\n  readonly text: string;\\n  readonly children: readonly UiNode[];\\n}\\n\\n/**\\n * How a step finds its target.\\n *\\n * Structural rather than textual on purpose: matching on visible text would make the family a string\\n * exercise, and text is the one thing that legitimately changes between record and replay.\\n */\\nexport interface Selector {\\n  readonly kind: \\\"testid\\\" | \\\"role_index\\\" | \\\"attr\\\";\\n  readonly value: string;\\n  /** For `attr`: which attribute. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Kz77DoD7x1fSLV3T5XYTLH","type":"tool_result","content":"==================== MANIFEST.json ====================\n{\n  \"familyId\": \"ui-action-record-replay\",\n  \"scenarioSetId\": \"replay-324-2255de40\",\n  \"visibleFiles\": [\n    \"README.md\",\n    \"SPEC.md\",\n    \"examples/example-1-none.json\",\n    \"examples/example-2-node_wrapped.json\",\n    \"examples/example-3-node_removed.json\",\n    \"starter/subject.mjs\",\n    \"types.ts\"\n  ],\n  \"hiddenArtifacts\": [\n    \"verify.ts\",\n    \"reference.ts\",\n    \"mutants.ts\",\n    \"runner.ts\",\n    \"app.ts\",\n    \"matrix.json\",\n    \"scenarios.json\"\n  ],\n  \"submissionFormat\": \"an ES module exporting `subject` with a replay(trace, app) method\"\n}\n\n==================== README.md ====================\n# Challenge: UI action record and replay\n\n## The problem\n\nA model can work out how to complete a flow in a web app. That is not the capability worth shipping.\nThe capability worth shipping is a **recording that replays deterministically, with no model in the\nloop** — because a workflow that needs the model on every run is a demo with a subscription.\n\nYou are given a recorded action trace and a live application. The page has changed since the\nrecording. Replay the trace, and either complete it or stop with a named reason.\n\n## What you are given\n\n| file | what it is |\n|---|---|\n| `SPEC.md` | the normative rules, the mutation space, and the facade contract |\n| `types.ts` | the domain model. Read-only. |\n| `starter/subject.mjs` | the interface you must implement, with a stub |\n| `examples/` | worked scenarios with the outcome each one should reach |\n\n## What you must produce\n\n```js\nexport const subject = {\n  id: \"my-implementation\",\n  label: \"...\",\n  replay(trace, app) {\n    // return { traceId, outcome, steps, unreplayableReason }\n  },\n};\n```\n\n`replay` may be called **twice with the same trace**. The second call must not repeat an\nirreversible step.\n\n## How you are graded\n\nHidden scenarios drawn from the same declared mutation space as the examples. Every mutation kind\nthey use is listed in `SPEC.md`.\n\nGraded on: the flow completes when it can, no irreversible effect fires when it should not, a trace\nthat can no longer be replayed is *reported as such* rather than half-run, the replay is idempotent,\nthe audit records what was observed rather than that a step happened — and **you never call\n`app.askModel`**. It is available, it works, and using it fails the run.\n\n**Refusing everything fails.** Declaring every trace unreplayable is safe and completes no work.\n\n==================== SPEC.md ====================\n# Replay specification (normative)\n\n## The rules\n\n| code | rule |\n|---|---|\n| `R1_SELECTOR_RESOLVED_LIVE` | Every step's selector must be resolved against the live tree at replay time. The recorded node id is a hint, not an address. |\n| `R2_PRECONDITION_OBSERVED` | Before a step runs, its precondition must be OBSERVED — read the attribute, do not assume the recorded value still holds. The audit records what was observed. |\n| `R3_POSTCONDITION_OBSERVED` | After a step runs, its postcondition is recorded from what the application shows, not from what the recording expected. |\n| `R4_CONFIRMATION_OBSERVED` | A step whose target declares `data-opens-confirmation` must not complete until a confirmation has been observed and accepted. An absent dialog is not a confirmation. |\n| `R5_IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY` | Replaying a trace that already completed must not repeat an irreversible step. Keeping that record is your job; the harness does not keep it for you. |\n| `R6_NO_MODEL_IN_LOOP` | `app.askModel` may not be called. A replay that needs a model is not a replay. |\n| `R7_AUDIT_EXPLAINS` | Every step reports the node it resolved, the precondition it observed, and — when it stopped — a named halt reason. |\n\n## Outcomes\n\n`completed`, `halted`, `unreplayable`\n\n- `completed` — every step ran.\n- `halted` — the page is in a state this trace cannot proceed from **right now**. Try later.\n- `unreplayable` — the trace can never replay as recorded and must be re-recorded.\n\nThose are three different operational instructions and grading distinguishes them. Reporting\n`halted` for a trace whose target is gone tells an operator to wait for something that will never\nhappen.\n\n## The mutation space\n\nThe live tree may differ from the recorded one in exactly these ways, at any depth in the trace:\n\n| mutation | what it does |\n|---|---|\n| `none` | nothing changed |\n| `attribute_renamed` | the target's `data-testid` was renamed |\n| `node_reordered` | siblings were reordered |\n| `node_wrapped` | the target was wrapped in a new element and **re-created, so its id changed** |\n| `text_changed` | visible text changed |\n| `node_removed` | the target is gone |\n\nAlso varying: whether a confirmation dialog is `absent`, `present` or `suppressed`; whether an\nasync region has settled; and whether the trace is replayed once or twice.\n\nNothing outside this list happens. The page has changed since the\nrecording. Replay the trace, and either complete it or stop with a named reason.\n\n## What you are given\n\n| file | what it is |\n|---|---|\n| `SPEC.md` | the normative rules, the mutation space, and the facade contract |\n| `types.ts` | the domain model. Read-only. |\n| `starter/subject.mjs` | the interface you must implement, with a stub |\n| `examples/` | worked scenarios with the outcome each one should reach |\n\n## What you must produce\n\n```js\nexport const subject = {\n  id: \"my-implementation\",\n  label: \"...\",\n  replay(trace, app) {\n    // return { traceId, outcome, steps, unreplayableReason }\n  },\n};\n```\n\n`replay` may be called **twice with the same trace**. The second call must not repeat an\nirreversible step.\n\n## How you are graded\n\nHidden scenarios drawn from the same declared mutation space as the examples. Every mutation kind\nthey use is listed in `SPEC.md`.\n\nGraded on: the flow completes when it can, no irreversible effect fires when it should not, a trace\nthat can no longer be replayed is *reported as such* rather than half-run, the replay is idempotent,\nthe audit records what was observed rather than that a step happened — and **you never call\n`app.askModel`**. It is available, it works, and using it fails the run.\n\n**Refusing everything fails.** Declaring every trace unreplayable is safe and completes no work.\n\n==================== SPEC.md ====================\n# Replay specification (normative)\n\n## The rules\n\n| code | rule |\n|---|---|\n| `R1_SELECTOR_RESOLVED_LIVE` | Every step's selector must be resolved against the live tree at replay time. The recorded node id is a hint, not an address. |\n| `R2_PRECONDITION_OBSERVED` | Before a step runs, its precondition must be OBSERVED — read the attribute, do not assume the recorded value still holds. The audit records what was observed. |\n| `R3_POSTCONDITION_OBSERVED` | After a step runs, its postcondition is recorded from what the application shows, not from what the recording expected. |\n| `R4_CONFIRMATION_OBSERVED` | A step whose target declares `data-opens-confirmation` must not complete until a confirmation has been observed and accepted. An absent dialog is not a confirmation. |\n| `R5_IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY` | Replaying a trace that already completed must not repeat an irreversible step. Keeping that record is your job; the harness does not keep it for you. |\n| `R6_NO_MODEL_IN_LOOP` | `app.askModel` may not be called. A replay that needs a model is not a replay. |\n| `R7_AUDIT_EXPLAINS` | Every step reports the node it resolved, the precondition it observed, and — when it stopped — a named halt reason. |\n\n## Outcomes\n\n`completed`, `halted`, `unreplayable`\n\n- `completed` — every step ran.\n- `halted` — the page is in a state this trace cannot proceed from **right now**. Try later.\n- `unreplayable` — the trace can never replay as recorded and must be re-recorded.\n\nThose are three different operational instructions and grading distinguishes them. Reporting\n`halted` for a trace whose target is gone tells an operator to wait for something that will never\nhappen.\n\n## The mutation space\n\nThe live tree may differ from the recorded one in exactly these ways, at any depth in the trace:\n\n| mutation | what it does |\n|---|---|\n| `none` | nothing changed |\n| `attribute_renamed` | the target's `data-testid` was renamed |\n| `node_reordered` | siblings were reordered |\n| `node_wrapped` | the target was wrapped in a new element and **re-created, so its id changed** |\n| `text_changed` | visible text changed |\n| `node_removed` | the target is gone |\n\nAlso varying: whether a confirmation dialog is `absent`, `present` or `suppressed`; whether an\nasync region has settled; and whether the trace is replayed once or twice.\n\nNothing outside this list happens. A hidden scenario samples this space more densely than the\nexamples do and adds no new kind of change.\n\n## The facade\n\n```\nquery(selector) -> { node, matches, pending }\nattr(nodeId, name) -> string | null\nclick(nodeId) / type(nodeId, text) / submit(nodeId)\nconfirmationPresent() -> boolean\nacceptConfirmation()   // throws when none is presented\naskModel(question)     // works, recorded, and a rule violation\n```\n\n`query` returns `matches` so ambiguity is visible: more than one match means the selector no\nlonger identifies one node, and picking the first is a guess.\n\n`pending: true` means the region exists and has not finished loading — *not yet*, as opposed to\n*not there*. Implement `replay` and export `subject`.\n//\n//   replay(trace, app) -> { traceId, outcome, steps, unreplayableReason }\n//\n// step audit: { index, resolvedNodeId, preconditionObserved, postconditionObserved,\n//               confirmationObserved, ran, haltReason }\n\nexport const subject = {\n  id: \"starter\",\n  label: \"Starter stub — replace me\",\n\n  replay(trace, app) {\n    const steps = [];\n\n    for (const step of trace.steps) {\n      // TODO: resolve the selector LIVE. step.recordedNodeId is a hint and may be stale.\n      const { node, matches, pending } = app.query(step.selector);\n\n      if (node === null) {\n        steps.push({\n          index: step.index,\n          resolvedNodeId: null,\n          preconditionObserved: null,\n          postconditionObserved: null,\n          confirmationObserved: null,\n          ran: false,\n          haltReason: pending ? \"REGION_PENDING\" : \"SELECTOR_UNRESOLVED\",\n        });\n        return { traceId: trace.id, outcome: \"halted\", steps, unreplayableReason: null };\n      }\n\n      // TODO: observe the precondition, handle confirmations, guard irreversible steps.\n      steps.push({\n        index: step.index,\n        resolvedNodeId: node.id,\n        preconditionObserved: null,\n        postconditionObserved: null,\n        confirmationObserved: null,\n        ran: false,\n        haltReason: \"NOT_IMPLEMENTED\",\n      });\n      return { traceId: trace.id, outcome: \"halted\", steps, unreplayableReason: null };\n    }\n\n    return { traceId: trace.id, outcome: \"completed\", steps, unreplayableReason: null };\n  },\n};\n\n==================== types.ts ====================\n// Domain model for the challenge. Read-only — the grader uses these exact shapes.\n\n// The PUBLIC domain model for UI action record-and-replay.\n//\n// Shipped verbatim in the agent-facing package, so nothing here is ground truth. The scenario, its\n// mutations and the effect ledger live in `truth.ts`.\n//\n// THE THESIS\n//\n// A model can discover a UI workflow. That is not the capability worth having. The capability worth\n// having is a RECORDING that replays deterministically with no model in the loop — because a\n// workflow that needs the model every time is not automation, it is a demo with a subscription.\n//\n// So the task is not \"click the right thing\". It is: record a trace whose every step carries what\n// must be true before it and what must be true after it, then replay that trace against a UI that\n// has changed underneath you, and either complete it or halt with a named reason. Improvising is a\n// failure even when it works — `R6_NO_MODEL_IN_LOOP` is a rule, and the harness owns the channel\n// that would let you break it.\n\nexport interface UiNode {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly role: string;\n  readonly attrs: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;\n  readonly text: string;\n  readonly children: readonly UiNode[];\n}\n\n/**\n * How a step finds its target.\n *\n * Structural rather than textual on purpose: matching on visible text would make the family a string\n * exercise, and text is the one thing that legitimately changes between record and replay.\n */\nexport interface Selector {\n  readonly kind: \"testid\" | \"role_index\" | \"attr\";\n  readonly value: string;\n  /** For `attr`: which attribute. For `role_index`: the index within the role. */\n  readonly qualifier: string | null;\n}\n\nexport const ACTION_KINDS = [\"click\", \"type\", \"submit\", \"confirm\"] as const;\nexport type ActionKind = (typeof ACTION_KINDS)[number];\n\n/** What must hold before a step runs. A step with no precondition is a step that cannot halt safely. */\nexport interface Precondition {\n  readonly nodeExists: boolean;\n  /** Attribute the target must carry, and the value it must have. */\n  readonly attr: string | null;\n  readonly attrValue: string | null;\n}\n\n/** What must hold after. Observed, never assumed. */\nexport interface Postcondition {\n  readonly effect: string | null;\n  readonly attr: string | null;\n  readonly attrValue: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface RecordedStep {\n  readonly index: number;\n  readonly kind: ActionKind;\n  readonly selector: Selector;\n  /**\n   * The node id this step resolved to WHEN IT WAS RECORDED.\n   *\n   * Present because real recordings store it, and because it is the temptation the family exists to\n   * measure: replaying against this id is faster, simpler, and wrong the moment the page changes.\n   * A correct replayer treats it as a hint and resolves the selector live.\n   */\n  readonly recordedNodeId: string;\n  readonly value: string | null;\n  readonly precondition: Precondition;\n  readonly postcondition: Postcondition;\n  /** True when running this step twice would do the thing twice. */\n  readonly irreversible: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface ActionTrace {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly steps: readonly RecordedStep[];\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- what a replayer may do\n\nexport interface QueryResult {\n  readonly node: UiNode | null;\n  /** How many nodes matched. More than one is ambiguous and must not be resolved by guessing. */\n  readonly matches: number;\n  /**\n   * True when the region exists but has not finished loading.\n   *\n   * The distinction between \"not there yet\" and \"not there\" is the whole of `asyncSettled`, and it\n   * has to be readable: a replayer that cannot tell them apart must either halt on everything absent\n   * or re-record on everything absent, and both are wrong half the time. A real DOM exposes the same\n   * thing through `aria-busy`.\n   */\n  readonly pending: boolean;\n}\n\n/**\n * The application facade.\n *\n * `askModel` exists and works. Using it is a rule violation, and the harness records every call —\n * which is what makes \"no model in the loop\" a measurement rather than an honour system. A facade\n * that simply omitted the method would measure nothing: the interesting failure is an implementation\n * that reaches for a model when the deterministic path gets hard.\n */\nexport interface AppFacade {\n  /** Resolve a selector against the LIVE tree, right now. */\n  query: (selector: Selector) => QueryResult;\n  /** Read one attribute of a node, live. */\n  attr: (nodeId: string, name: string) => string | null;\n  click: (nodeId: string) => void;\n  type: (nodeId: string, text: string) => void;\n  submit: (nodeId: string) => void;\n  /** Observe whether a confirmation is currently presented. */\n  confirmationPresent: () => boolean;\n  /** Accept the presented confirmation. Throws if none is presented. */\n  acceptConfirmation: () => void;\n  /** Available, recorded, and a rule violation to call. */\n  askModel: (question: string) => string;\n}\n\nexport const OUTCOMES = [\"completed\", \"halted\", \"unreplayable\"] as const;\nexport type Outcome = (typeof OUTCOMES)[number];\n\nexport interface StepAudit {\n  readonly index: number;\n  /** The node id the selector actually resolved to at replay time, or null when it did not. */\n  readonly resolvedNodeId: string | null;\n  /** What the precondition check actually observed. Not \"ok\" — the observed value. */\n  readonly preconditionObserved: string | null;\n  readonly postconditionObserved: string | null;\n  readonly confirmationObserved: boolean | null;\n  readonly ran: boolean;\n  /** Why the step did not run, when it did not. Named reasons only. */\n  readonly haltReason: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface ReplayReport {\n  readonly traceId: string;\n  readonly outcome: Outcome;\n  readonly steps: readonly StepAudit[];\n  /** Set when the whole trace was refused as unreplayable, with the reason. */\n  readonly unreplayableReason: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface Subject {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly label: string;\n  /**\n   * Replay one recorded trace against a live app.\n   *\n   * Called once per replay. A scenario may call it twice with the same trace, which is the\n   * idempotency test: the second run must not repeat an irreversible step.\n   */\n  readonly replay: (trace: ActionTrace, app: AppFacade) => ReplayReport;\n}","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"dd2151b3-cfd7-4554-ad5b-90d5a473e752","uuid":"48ea6343-5079-41fe-ba67-194161c592b5","timestamp":"2026-09-05T07:58:18.220Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"==================== starter/subject.mjs ====================\n// Starter.
